## User

# Idasletten — Table Football Tournament App
 
> **Build conventions**
> - Use the folder name as app name if deploying to Fly.io.
>
 
---
 
## 1. Overview
 
Idasletten is a web app for running table‑football (foosball) tournaments: tracking players, teams, matches, results, and a configurable scoreboard. It has a Norse‑mythology theme.
 
Authentication is optional. Anyone can browse and record match results without logging in. Logging in (via Azure AD) is only required to **create tournaments** and to **edit already‑completed matches**.
 
---
 
## 2. Data model
 
### Tournament
| Field | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Name | string | |
| TeamSize | int | Players per team. Default **2**. |
| PointsToWin | int | Points (goals) needed to win a match. Default **5**. |
| ScoreSystem | enum | `Elo`, `TrueSkill`, `Lives`, `WinCount` |
| MaxPlayerCount | int? | Optional. Empty means that there is no limit to the number of players |
| IsArchived | bool | |
| IsPublic | bool | |
| SeedTournamentId | Guid? | Source tournament used to **seed/plan matches** in this tournament. |
| ParentTournamentId | Guid? | This tournament is a later **round** that continues from a previous tournament; the parent's results are used to create this tournament's players. A tournament may be **seeded only if it has no parent**. When viewing all tournaments, we should not view child tournaments by default. |
| RoundNumber | int? | Only relevant when `ParentTournamentId` is set. Default is 1 and this is autoincremented when a tournament is created from a parent. |

 
### TournamentPlayer
| Field | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| UserId | Guid | |
| TournamentId | Guid | |
| Score | double | Value depends on the tournament's `ScoreSystem`. |
| WinCount | int | |
| MatchCount | int | |
| LoseCount | int | |
| Lives | int | Only meaningful when `ScoreSystem = Lives`. Should only be set when the tournament uses the `Lives` scoring system. Default is 3 |
| PointsWon | int | Points is the same as goals. This nameing is selected to support other scoring systems or game types |
| PointsLost | int | |
| ScoreDiff | double | Change in `Score` since the player's last match. Display delta. Ex +12 or -12 |
 
### TournamentTeam
Has one or more `TournamentPlayer`s.
| Field | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Name | string | This is not an input field, but autogenerated Team 1, Team 2 etc. Maybe overridden in a future feature  |
| Number | int | This is not an input field, but autogenerated 1, 2 etc |
 
### TournamentMatch
Has two or more teams. Usually just two, but the spec allows more for flexibility.
| Field | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Order | int | Sequence/display ordering. |
| TournamentId | Guid | |
| State | enum | `Planned`, `Done`, `Cancelled`. |

 
### TournamentTeamMatchResult
| Field | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| MatchId | Guid | |
| TournamentId | Guid | |
| TeamId | Guid | |
| GoalsWon | int | |
| GoalsLost | int | |
 
### User
A user can join many tournaments. Use as many standard .net identity fields as possible.
| Field | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Username | string | Usually 3 initials. This is should be unique. |
| Name | string | |
| Email | string | This is optional, because a user may not have an email address. |
| ImageUrl | string | Fetched via the Azure **Graph API**. Get all images from a organisation (Mjølner is a company ). This should be fetched when a user is created |

 
---
 
## 3. Scoring systems  *(largest open area)*
 
The tournament's `ScoreSystem` determines how `TournamentPlayer.Score` is calculated. The spec lists four systems but does not define their rules. Each needs to be specified:
 
- **Elo** — Use normal ELO scoring mechanism. When the teams consists of multiple players, then take an average score on a team. 
- **TrueSkill** — Use the Moserware.Skills https://github.com/moserware/Skills library to calculate the TrueSkill score. [Samples](https://github.com/moserware/Skills/blob/master/Sample/Content/TrueSkillSamples.cs.pp)
- **Lives** — When a game is lost, then you lose a life. .
- **WinCount** — presumably `Score = WinCount`. Confirm tie‑breakers (goal difference?).
 
---
 
## 4. Pages
 
### `/` — Home
- A large hero image and this text:
  > *"Idasletten (Iðavöllr) findes i nordisk mytologi lige uden for Valhals porte. Det er her vikingerne som er døde i krig udkæmper deres drabelige slag hver dag, som øvelse til Ragnarok. Hver aften er Idasletten rød af blod."*
- A selector for **public** tournaments. This shows all none archived tournaments that have `IsPublic = true`. Each tournament is a clickable card that navigates to its detail page.
- A (deliberately less prominent) link to historical tournaments (shows archived and private tournaments).
 
### `/tournaments` — All tournaments
- Lists all historical tournaments.
- A **Create tournament** button (requires login).
 
### `/tournaments/{tournamentId}` — Tournament detail
**Main area — scoreboard**
- Table of `TournamentPlayer`s showing won, lost, and `Score` (per the selected score system).
- An **Add player** button at the top opens a dialog to add a player to the tournament. Adding a player by initials that haven't been used before also creates a `User`.
 
**Right column (≈ ¼ width)**
- Top: **Create match** button → navigates to `/tournaments/{tournamentId}/create-match`.
- Card: **next 5 planned matches** (if any). Each is clickable and navigates to the create‑match page **pre‑filled** with that match's data.
- Card: **recent 5 played matches** (if any). Each is clickable and navigates to that match's detail page (same as create‑match but read‑only). 
- Below the cards: a **View all matches** link → `/tournaments/{tournamentId}/matches`.
 
### `/tournaments/{tournamentId}/create-match`
- Inputs for the **initials** of each team's players, plus the score.
- **No login required.**
- Match name and team names are **not** input fields (auto‑derived — see model clarifications).
- Unregistered initials are allowed and auto‑create the players (and a user if not already defined).
- Below the initials input: a **Select from list** link that opens a dialog of existing players (checkboxes).
- Suggestion from the original: generate the match `Id` before navigating here so the same page can be reused for editing a planned match.
 
### `/tournaments/{tournamentId}/matches`
Two tables:
- **Planned matches** — with two actions:
  - **Add planned match** — dialog to enter the players' initials for one match.
  - **Plan several matches** — dialog (see fields below).
- **Match results** — completed matches.
 
**"Plan several matches" dialog fields**
- *Plan based on a previous tournament* — optional; shows the seed tournament (selectable if not already set).
- *Number of games per player.* This is used to calculate how many matches should be created.
- *Fixed team* — should teams reshuffle after each match?
- *Seeding type:*
  - **Random** — teams chosen randomly.
  - **Equality** — using the seed tournament, pair best with worst (1+N, 2+(N−1), …).
  - **Fair** — split ranked players into a top half and a bottom half, then pair the best of the top half with the best of the bottom half, and so on. *Example given:* 10 players → 1+6, 2+7, 3+8, 4+9, 5+10.
 
### `/tournaments/create`
Configure: Name, MaxPlayerCount (optional), TeamSize (default 2), PointsToWin (default 5), ScoreSystem (dropdown — consider an info icon explaining each system), IsPublic.
- A **Create** button.
- A **Create and Plan** link below it. **⚠️ CLARIFY:** where does it navigate after creating — `/plan-match`, `/matches`, or `/players`?
 
### `/tournaments/{tournamentId}/players`
- A players table with an **Add a player** button at the top:
  - **Add player** — dialog for initials and an optional name.
- Below the table, an **Add players based on a previous tournament** section:
  - If no seed tournament is set, the section is just a menu button listing all previous tournaments.
  - Once a tournament is selected, it lists that tournament's players ordered by their score there, each with a **+ / −** button. Clicking **+** adds that player to the current tournament and strikes through their name in the list.
 
### `/users/{userId}`
Cross‑tournament stats for a single player.
 
### `/login`
- Azure AD login via an app registration.
- A **second, test‑only login option** shown next to the Microsoft button — see §6 (Testing).
 
---
 
## 5. Multi‑round / seeding flow

A tournament can consist of multiple rounds, each represented as a separate tournament with `ParentTournamentId` linking to the previous round. Players from the parent tournament carry over to the child tournament, but their scores reset. This allows for a flow where players compete in an initial tournament, then the top N players advance to a second tournament (round 2), and so on. The `RoundNumber` field tracks which round a tournament is in.

The seed tournament can be used on a single round tournament or tournament needs multiple rounds.
 
---
 
## 6. Authentication & permissions
 
- Login is **optional** for general use.
- **Login required for:** creating a tournament; editing a match that is already `Done` (which require recalculation of match results)
- **No login required for:** recording a new match result (`/create-match`).
 
**Forwarded headers (Fly.io):** Configure `UseForwardedHeaders` with `KnownNetworks` and `KnownProxies` **cleared**, so ASP.NET Core trusts Fly's proxy and generates `https://` redirect URIs for Azure AD.
 
---
 
## 7. Tech stack & architecture
 
**Stack**
- C#, Entity Framework Core, **SQLite**.
- Razor Pages for rendering.
- **basecoat** UI via CDN (https://basecoatui.com/installation/#install-cdn). Do **not** install Tailwind as it is included in the CDN bundle. Components reference: https://basecoatui.com/kitchen-sink/.
- Flexbox for layout.
- A **light ("white") theme** — not dark.
 
**Local database**
Use **SQLite in‑memory mode** locally (works with migrations + seeding) and a file‑based SQLite database in production. Confirm this interpretation.
 
**Migrations**
- Always create migrations with the `dotnet ef` CLI.
- Apply migrations automatically on app startup. *(Record this rule in `AGENTS.md`.)*
 
**Deployment**
- Deploy to Fly.io using the `fly` CLI.
- Also add a **GitHub Action** that deploys `main` to Fly.io on push.
 
**Projects**
- `Idasletten`
- `Idasletten.Tests`
 
**Architecture — CQRS + vertical slices (MediatR)**
 
Folders inside `Idasletten`:
- **Pages** — Razor pages with minimal logic; they only send commands/queries via MediatR.
- **Features** — one folder per feature/slice, each containing its handlers, commands, queries, entities, etc. Within a slice, commands and queries get their own sub‑folders. Handlers may use `DbContext` directly (avoid repositories/services). **Every command handler publishes an event at the end** (e.g. `CreateUserHandler` → `UserCreated`). 
- **Shared** — anything not specific to one feature.
 
---
 
## 8. Testing
 
- Integration/feature tests use a **custom `WebApplicationFactory`** with an **in‑memory database**.
- The factory **seeds data** both when running tests and when running the app locally.
- **Test data factories:** a static `Any` class in the test project with methods like `Any.User()` that initialise all fields with random values.
- **xUnit**, **AAA** pattern (Arrange / Act / Assert).
- Test method naming: `Should_DoSomething_When_ConditionIsFulfilled`.
- Prefer **simple stubs** over mocking frameworks when a dependency is needed.
- **Test‑user login:** a second login option (next to Microsoft Azure), enabled **only** when both `TestUser__Password` and `TestUser__Email` env vars are set. This user is also seeded into the database.
 
---
 
## 9. Required deliverables
 
- **`AGENTS.md`** documenting the tech stack, architecture, rules, and hints — including how to run and test locally, and the "migrations auto‑apply on startup" rule.
- **IMPORTANT! Do UI/design validation with Playwright screenshots** via the Playwright **MCP server**, authenticating with the test user to view all pages. *Do not write Playwright unit/integration tests* — screenshots for visual validation only.

## 3. DATA MODEL (Entities, Enums, DbContext)

### Enums:

**ScoreSystem** (in `Tournament.cs`):
```csharp
public enum ScoreSystem { Elo, TrueSkill, Lives, WinCount }
```

**MatchState** (in `TournamentMatch.cs`):
```csharp
public enum MatchState { Planned, Done, Cancelled }
```

**SeedingType** (in `PlanSeveralMatches.cs`):
```csharp
public enum SeedingType { Random, Equality, Fair }
```

### Entity Relationship Diagram:

```
User (1) ──< TournamentPlayer >── (1) Tournament
                    │                      │
                    │                      │
            TournamentTeamPlayer    TournamentTeam ──< TournamentTeamMatchResult >── TournamentMatch
                    │                      │
                    └── TournamentTeam ─────┘
```

### Entities:

| Entity | Key Fields | Navigation Properties |
|--------|-----------|----------------------|
| **User** | `Id` (Guid), `Username` (unique, max 20), `Name` (max 200), `Email?`, `ImageUrl?`, `CreatedAt` | `TournamentPlayers` |
| **Tournament** | `Id` (Guid), `Name` (max 200), `TeamSize` (default 2), `PointsToWin` (default 5), `ScoreSystem`, `MaxPlayerCount?`, `IsArchived`, `IsPublic`, `SeedTournamentId?`, `ParentTournamentId?`, `RoundNumber?`, `CreatedAt` | `SeedTournament?`, `ParentTournament?`, `Players`, `Matches`, `Teams` |
| **TournamentPlayer** | `Id` (Guid), `UserId`, `TournamentId` (unique combo index), `Score` (default 1000), `WinCount`, `MatchCount`, `LoseCount`, `Lives` (default 3), `PointsWon`, `PointsLost`, `ScoreDiff` | `User`, `Tournament`, `TeamPlayers` |
| **TournamentTeam** | `Id` (Guid), `Name` ("Team 1", "Team 2"), `Number`, `TournamentId` | `Tournament`, `TeamPlayers`, `MatchResults` |
| **TournamentTeamPlayer** (join) | Composite PK: `(TournamentTeamId, TournamentPlayerId)` | `Team`, `Player` |
| **TournamentMatch** | `Id` (Guid), `Order`, `TournamentId`, `State`, `CreatedAt`, `PlayedAt?` | `Tournament`, `TeamResults` |
| **TournamentTeamMatchResult** | `Id` (Guid), `MatchId`, `TournamentId`, `TeamId`, `GoalsWon`, `GoalsLost` | `Match`, `Team` |

### AppDbContext (SQLite):
- **7 DbSets**: `Users`, `Tournaments`, `TournamentPlayers`, `TournamentTeams`, `TournamentTeamPlayers`, `TournamentMatches`, `TournamentTeamMatchResults`
- **OnModelCreating** configures all keys, indexes, foreign keys, cascade deletes, max lengths
- Key constraint: `TournamentTeamMatchResult.TeamId` uses `DeleteBehavior.NoAction` (to avoid multiple cascade paths)

---

## 4. PROGRAM.CS AND APPSETTINGS CONFIGURATION

### Program.cs Key Points:
1. **Forwarded Headers**: Configured for Fly.io proxy (clears KnownNetworks/KnownProxies for `XForwardedFor` + `XForwardedProto`)
2. **Razor Pages**: `AddRazorPages()` with static assets
3. **MediatR**: `AddMediatR(cfg => cfg.RegisterServicesFromAssembly(typeof(Program).Assembly))` -- scans main assembly
4. **Database**: SQLite with connection string from config or fallback to `idasletten.db` in content root
5. **Auth**: Conditional -- if `AzureAd:ClientId` is configured, uses OpenIdConnect + Cookie; otherwise just Cookie auth
6. **Migrations**: Auto-applies on startup in non-Testing environments + runs `DatabaseSeeder.SeedAsync()`
7. **Test User**: Seeds a test user if `TestUser:Email` and `TestUser:Password` are configured
8. **Middleware Pipeline**: ForwardedHeaders → ExceptionHandler/HSTS → HTTPS → StaticFiles → Routing → Auth → MapRazorPages

### appsettings.json:
```json
{
  "ConnectionStrings": { "DefaultConnection": "DataSource=idasletten.db" },
  "AzureAd": { "TenantId": "", "ClientId": "", "ClientSecret": "" },
  "TestUser": { "Email": "", "Password": "" }
}
```

### appsettings.Development.json:
```json
{ "DetailedErrors": true, "Logging": {...} }
```

### launchSettings.json:
- **http**: `http://localhost:5021`
- **https**: `https://localhost:7045;http://localhost:5021`

---

## 5. PAGES STRUCTURE

| Page | Route | Auth Required? | Code-Behind Logic |
|------|-------|----------------|-------------------|
| **Index** | `/` | No | Sends `GetPublicTournamentsQuery` via MediatR |
| **Login** | `/Login` | No | Shows Azure AD sign-in OR test login form; `OnPostTestLoginAsync` creates cookie claims |
| **Error** | (default) | No | Standard error page |
| **Privacy** | (default) | No | Placeholder |
| **Account/Logout** | (default) | No | Signs out cookie auth, redirects to `/` |
| **Tournaments/Index** | `/Tournaments` | No | Sends `GetTournamentsQuery(IncludeArchived: true)` |
| **Tournaments/Create** | `/Tournaments/Create` | **Yes** `[Authorize]` | Sends `CreateTournamentCommand`; option to "Create & Plan Matches" |
| **Tournaments/Detail** | `/tournaments/{tournamentId}` | No | Sends `GetTournamentByIdQuery` + `GetMatchesForTournamentQuery`; has "Add Player" dialog |
| **Tournaments/CreateMatch** | `/tournaments/{tournamentId}/create-match` | No | Sends `RecordMatchResultCommand`; supports editing existing matches via `?matchId=` |
| **Tournaments/Matches** | `/tournaments/{tournamentId}/matches` | No | Shows planned/completed matches; dialogs for "Add planned", "Plan several", seed from other tournament |
| **Tournaments/MatchDetail** | `/tournaments/{tournamentId}/matches/{matchId}` | No | Shows match result; edit link shown if authenticated |
| **Tournaments/Players** | `/tournaments/{tournamentId}/players` | No | Shows player list; add player dialog; seed from previous tournament |
| **Users/Profile** | `/users/{userId}` | No | Sends `GetUserByIdQuery`, shows stats summary + tournament history |

All code-behind files follow the pattern: inject `IMediator`, send commands/queries, keep logic minimal.

---

## 6. FEATURES / SLICES ALREADY IMPLEMENTED

### Complete Feature Inventory:

| Feature Slice | Commands | Queries | Events (Notifications) |
|---------------|----------|---------|----------------------|
| **Matches** | `CreatePlannedMatchCommand` → creates a planned match with auto-created users/players/teams | `GetMatchesForTournamentQuery` → returns `MatchesResult(Planned, Completed)` | `PlannedMatchCreated`, `SeveralMatchesPlanned`, `MatchResultRecorded` |
| | `PlanSeveralMatchesCommand` → generates multiple planned matches with 3 seeding algorithms | `GetMatchByIdQuery` → returns full match with teams/players/users | |
| | `RecordMatchResultCommand` → records/updates a match with goals, applies scoring | | |
| **Players** | `AddPlayerToTournamentCommand` → creates user if needed, adds to tournament | *(none)* | `PlayerAddedToTournament` |
| **Tournaments** | `CreateTournamentCommand` → creates tournament with optional parent/seed | `GetTournamentsQuery`, `GetPublicTournamentsQuery`, `GetTournamentByIdQuery` | `TournamentCreated` |
| **Users** | `CreateUserCommand` → creates a user | `GetUserByUsernameQuery`, `GetUserByIdQuery` | `UserCreated` |
| **Scoring** | *(none -- these are services, not commands)* | *(none)* | *(none)* |

### Scoring Systems (Strategy pattern via `ScoringServiceFactory`):
1. **EloScoringService**: Standard Elo with team average, K=32
2. **TrueSkillScoringService**: Bayesian via `Moserware.Skills`, score = `(Mean - 3*StdDev) * 100`
3. **LivesScoringService**: Start with 3 lives, lose 1 per loss, score = remaining lives
4. **WinCountScoringService**: Score = win count, tie-breaker = goal diff

### Match Generation Algorithms (in `PlanSeveralMatchesHandler`):
- **Random**: Shuffles players randomly
- **Equality**: Pairs best vs worst (1+N, 2+(N-1)...)
- **Fair**: Top half vs bottom half

---

## 7. HOW MEDIATR IS SET UP

### Registration:
```csharp
// Program.cs, line 19:
builder.Services.AddMediatR(cfg => cfg.RegisterServicesFromAssembly(typeof(Program).Assembly));
```
This scans the entire `Idasletten` assembly for all `IRequest`, `IRequestHandler`, and `INotification` implementations.

### Pattern Used (every feature follows this):
1. **Command/Query** = a `record` implementing `IRequest<TResponse>` or `IRequest`
2. **Handler** = a class implementing `IRequestHandler<TRequest, TResponse>`, injected with `AppDbContext` and optionally `IMediator`
3. **Event** = a `record` implementing `INotification`, published at the end of each command handler via `_mediator.Publish()`
4. Handlers work directly with `AppDbContext` -- no repository layer

### Usage in Pages:
```csharp
// Inject IMediator, send commands/queries:
PublicTournaments = await _mediator.Send(new GetPublicTournamentsQuery());
await _mediator.Send(new CreateTournamentCommand(...));
await _mediator.Send(new RecordMatchResultCommand(...));
```

### Event Notification Records Defined:
- `TournamentCreated(Guid TournamentId)`
- `UserCreated(Guid UserId, string Username)`
- `PlayerAddedToTournament(Guid PlayerId, Guid TournamentId)`
- `PlannedMatchCreated(Guid MatchId, Guid TournamentId)`
- `SeveralMatchesPlanned(Guid TournamentId, int Count)`
- `MatchResultRecorded(Guid MatchId, Guid TournamentId)`

---

## 8. AGENTS.MD

**Located at**: `C:\mjolner-code\ai\idasletten-llm-benchmark-v2\idasletten-cp-sonnet-v2\AGENTS.md`

This is a comprehensive 171-line development guide for AI agents. Key rules documented:

1. **Tech Stack**: .NET 9, Razor Pages, EF Core + SQLite, MediatR CQRS, basecoat UI (CDN, includes Tailwind), Azure AD auth, xUnit testing
2. **Migrations**: Must use `dotnet ef migrations add <Name>`, auto-apply on startup
3. **Architecture**: Vertical slices, minimal page logic, handlers use DbContext directly, every command publishes an event
4. **Auth Rules**: Create-match is public; Tournament create requires `[Authorize]`
5. **Test Rules**: Naming `Should_DoSomething_When_Condition`, use `Any.*()` factories, InMemory DB
6. **UI**: basecoat via CDN, light theme, Inter font, Flexbox
7. **Deployment**: Fly.io with `fly.toml`, Dockerfile-based
8. **Test User**: Configure via `TestUser:Email` / `TestUser:Password` for local dev

---

## 9. TEST PROJECT STRUCTURE

```
Idasletten.Tests/
├── Any.cs                                    # Static factory for test entities
├── IdaslettenWebApplicationFactory.cs        # Custom WebApplicationFactory<Program>
├── Features/
│   └── Scoring/
│       ├── EloScoringServiceTests.cs         # 3 tests: win increases score, matchCount increments, underdog gains more
│       ├── LivesScoringServiceTests.cs       # 2 tests: losing costs a life, lives floor at 0
│       └── WinCountScoringServiceTests.cs    # 1 test: winCount increments on win
└── Integration/
    └── HomePageTests.cs                      # 3 integration tests: home page returns 200, tournaments page returns 200, create redirects unauthenticated
```

### Test Infrastructure:

**IdaslettenWebApplicationFactory**:
- Uses `WebApplicationFactory<Program>` (the `public partial class Program` in Program.cs enables this)
- Sets environment to `"Testing"` (which skips SQLite migrations in Program.cs)
- Removes all EF Core-related service registrations
- Replaces with InMemory database (`UseInMemoryDatabase`), unique per test run (via `Guid.NewGuid()`)
- Has a `SeedData(Action<AppDbContext>)` helper

**Any.cs** (Test Data Factory):
- `Any.User(username?)` -- random 3-letter initials
- `Any.Tournament(name?, scoreSystem?, isPublic?)` -- random name
- `Any.TournamentPlayer(userId, tournamentId)` -- random stats within sensible ranges
- Always uses `Guid.NewGuid()` for IDs, never hardcoded

**Test Framework**: xUnit with `IClassFixture<IdaslettenWebApplicationFactory>` for integration tests.

---

### Summary

This is a well-structured, production-ready **ASP.NET Core 9 Razor Pages** application for managing foosball tournaments with Norse mythology theming. It follows **Vertical Slice Architecture** with **MediatR CQRS**, uses **SQLite + EF Core** for persistence, supports **four scoring systems** (Elo, TrueSkill, Lives, WinCount), has **Azure AD authentication** with optional test login, is **Dockerized** for Fly.io deployment, and includes both **unit tests** (scoring algorithms) and **integration tests** (HTTP endpoints via WebApplicationFactory). Every command handler publishes domain events, and all page code-behind files are thin mediators between the UI and the CQRS handlers.
</task_result>
</task>

{
  "content": "using Idasletten.Shared.Entities;\n\nnamespace Idasletten.Features.Scoring;\n\npublic class EloScoringService : IScoringService\n{\n    private const double K = 32;\n\n    public void Calculate(\n        TournamentMatch match,\n        IReadOnlyList<TournamentTeamMatchResult> teamResults,\n        IReadOnlyList<TournamentTeam> teams,\n        IReadOnlyList<TournamentPlayer> players)\n    {\n        var team1Result = teamResults[0];\n        var team2Result = teamResults[1];\n        var team1 = teams.Single(t => t.Id == team1Result.TeamId);\n        var team2 = teams.Single(t => t.Id == team2Result.TeamId);\n\n        var team1Players = GetPlayers(team1, players);\n        var team2Players = GetPlayers(team2, players);\n\n        var team1AvgScore = team1Players.Average(p => p.Score);\n        var team2AvgScore = team2Players.Average(p => p.Score);\n\n        var team1Expected = 1.0 / (1.0 + Math.Pow(10, (team2AvgScore - team1AvgScore) / 400.0));\n        var team2Expected = 1.0 - team1Expected;\n\n        var team1Won = team1Result.GoalsWon > team2Result.GoalsWon;\n        var team1Actual = team1Won ? 1.0 : 0.0;\n        var team2Actual = 1.0 - team1Actual;\n\n        var scoreChange1 = K * (team1Actual - team1Expected);\n        var scoreChange2 = K * (team2Actual - team2Expected);\n\n        foreach (var p in team1Players)\n        {\n            p.ScoreDiff = scoreChange1;\n            p.Score += scoreChange1;\n        }\n\n        foreach (var p in team2Players)\n        {\n            p.ScoreDiff = scoreChange2;\n            p.Score += scoreChange2;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static List<TournamentPlayer> GetPlayers(TournamentTeam team, IReadOnlyList<TournamentPlayer> allPlayers)\n    {\n        return team.TeamPlayers\n            .Select(tp => allPlayers.Single(p => p.Id == tp.TournamentPlayerId))\n            .ToList();\n    }\n}\n",

}

{
  "content": "using Idasletten.Shared.Entities;\n\nnamespace Idasletten.Features.Scoring;\n\npublic class LivesScoringService : IScoringService\n{\n    public void Calculate(\n        TournamentMatch match,\n        IReadOnlyList<TournamentTeamMatchResult> teamResults,\n        IReadOnlyList<TournamentTeam> teams,\n        IReadOnlyList<TournamentPlayer> players)\n    {\n        var team1Result = teamResults[0];\n        var team2Result = teamResults[1];\n        var team1 = teams.Single(t => t.Id == team1Result.TeamId);\n        var team2 = teams.Single(t => t.Id == team2Result.TeamId);\n\n        var team1Players = GetPlayers(team1, players);\n        var team2Players = GetPlayers(team2, players);\n\n        var team1Won = team1Result.GoalsWon > team2Result.GoalsWon;\n\n        foreach (var p in team1Players)\n        {\n            if (!team1Won && p.Lives > 0)\n            {\n                p.ScoreDiff = -1;\n                p.Score -= 1;\n                p.Lives--;\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                p.ScoreDiff = 0;\n            }\n        }\n\n        foreach (var p in team2Players)\n        {\n            if (team1Won && p.Lives > 0)\n            {\n                p.ScoreDiff = -1;\n                p.Score -= 1;\n                p.Lives--;\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                p.ScoreDiff = 0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static List<TournamentPlayer> GetPlayers(TournamentTeam team, IReadOnlyList<TournamentPlayer> allPlayers)\n    {\n        return team.TeamPlayers\n            .Select(tp => allPlayers.Single(p => p.Id == tp.TournamentPlayerId))\n            .ToList();\n    }\n}\n",

{
  "content": "using Idasletten.Shared.Entities;\n\nnamespace Idasletten.Features.Scoring;\n\npublic class WinCountScoringService : IScoringService\n{\n    public void Calculate(\n        TournamentMatch match,\n        IReadOnlyList<TournamentTeamMatchResult> teamResults,\n        IReadOnlyList<TournamentTeam> teams,\n        IReadOnlyList<TournamentPlayer> players)\n    {\n        var team1Result = teamResults[0];\n        var team2Result = teamResults[1];\n        var team1 = teams.Single(t => t.Id == team1Result.TeamId);\n        var team2 = teams.Single(t => t.Id == team2Result.TeamId);\n\n        var team1Won = team1Result.GoalsWon > team2Result.GoalsWon;\n\n        foreach (var p in GetPlayers(team1, players))\n        {\n            if (team1Won)\n            {\n                p.WinCount++;\n                p.Score++;\n                p.ScoreDiff = 1;\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                p.ScoreDiff = 0;\n            }\n        }\n\n        foreach (var p in GetPlayers(team2, players))\n        {\n            if (!team1Won)\n            {\n                p.WinCount++;\n                p.Score++;\n                p.ScoreDiff = 1;\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                p.ScoreDiff = 0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static List<TournamentPlayer> GetPlayers(TournamentTeam team, IReadOnlyList<TournamentPlayer> allPlayers)\n    {\n        return team.TeamPlayers\n            .Select(tp => allPlayers.Single(p => p.Id == tp.TournamentPlayerId))\n            .ToList();\n    }\n}\n",

{
  "content": "using Idasletten.Shared.Entities;\nusing Moserware.Skills;\n\nnamespace Idasletten.Features.Scoring;\n\npublic class TrueSkillScoringService : IScoringService\n{\n    public void Calculate(\n        TournamentMatch match,\n        IReadOnlyList<TournamentTeamMatchResult> teamResults,\n        IReadOnlyList<TournamentTeam> teams,\n        IReadOnlyList<TournamentPlayer> players)\n    {\n        var team1Result = teamResults[0];\n        var team2Result = teamResults[1];\n        var team1 = teams.Single(t => t.Id == team1Result.TeamId);\n        var team2 = teams.Single(t => t.Id == team2Result.TeamId);\n\n        var gameInfo = GameInfo.DefaultGameInfo;\n        var team1Won = team1Result.GoalsWon > team2Result.GoalsWon;\n\n        foreach (var player in players)\n        {\n            var prevScore = player.Score / 100.0;\n            var playerRating = new Rating(prevScore * 2.5 + 25, prevScore * 2.5 + 8.333);\n            playerRating = null; // placeholder - TrueSkill uses its own rating state\n        }\n\n        var team1Players = GetRatingPlayers(team1, players);\n        var team2Players = GetRatingPlayers(team2, players);\n\n        var teamsArray = new[]\n        {\n            new Team(team1Players[0]),\n            new Team(team2Players[0])\n        };\n\n        if (team1Players.Count > 1)\n            teamsArray[0] = new Team(team1Players[0], team1Players[1]);\n        if (team2Players.Count > 1)\n            teamsArray[1] = new Team(team2Players[0], team2Players[1]);\n\n        var ranks = team1Won ? new[] { 1, 2 } : new[] { 2, 1 };\n\n        var newRatings = TrueSkillCalculator.CalculateNewRatings(gameInfo, teamsArray, ranks);\n\n        UpdatePlayerScore(team1, players, newRatings, 0);\n        UpdatePlayerScore(team2, players, newRatings, 1);\n    }\n\n    private static List<Player> GetRatingPlayers(TournamentTeam team, IReadOnlyList<TournamentPlayer> allPlayers)\n    {\n        var result = new List<Player>();\n        foreach (var tp in team.TeamPlayers)\n        {\n            var player = allPlayers.Single(p => p.Id == tp.TournamentPlayerId);\n            var skill = player.Score / 100.0;\n            result.Add(new Player(skill));\n        }\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    private static void UpdatePlayerScore(\n        TournamentTeam team,\n        IReadOnlyList<TournamentPlayer> allPlayers,\n        IDictionary<Player, Rating> newRatings,\n        int teamIndex)\n    {\n        var teamPlayers = team.TeamPlayers.ToList();\n        foreach (var tp in teamPlayers)\n        {\n            var player = allPlayers.Single(p => p.Id == tp.TournamentPlayerId);\n            var skill = player.Score / 100.0;\n            var ratingPlayer = new Player(skill);\n            if (newRatings.TryGetValue(ratingPlayer, out var newRating))\n            {\n                var newScore = (newRating.Mean - 3 * newRating.StandardDeviation) * 100;\n                player.ScoreDiff = newScore - player.Score;\n                player.Score = newScore;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n",

  "content": "using Idasletten.Features.Tournaments.Queries;\nusing Idasletten.Shared.Entities;\nusing Idasletten.Shared.Infrastructure;\nusing MediatR;\nusing Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;\n\nnamespace Idasletten.Features.Tournaments.Queries;\n\npublic class GetTournamentsHandler :\n    IRequestHandler<GetTournamentsQuery, List<Tournament>>,\n    IRequestHandler<GetPublicTournamentsQuery, List<Tournament>>,\n    IRequestHandler<GetTournamentByIdQuery, Tournament?>\n{\n    private readonly AppDbContext _db;\n\n    public GetTournamentsHandler(AppDbContext db)\n    {\n        _db = db;\n    }\n\n    public async Task<List<Tournament>> Handle(GetTournamentsQuery query, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var q = _db.Tournaments.AsQueryable();\n        if (!query.IncludeArchived)\n            q = q.Where(t => !t.IsArchived && t.ParentTournamentId == null);\n        return await q.OrderByDescending(t => t.CreatedAt).ToListAsync(ct);\n    }\n\n    public async Task<List<Tournament>> Handle(GetPublicTournamentsQuery query, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        return await _db.Tournaments\n            .Where(t => t.IsPublic && !t.IsArchived && t.ParentTournamentId == null)\n            .OrderByDescending(t => t.CreatedAt)\n            .ToListAsync(ct);\n    }\n\n    public async Task<Tournament?> Handle(GetTournamentByIdQuery query, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        return await _db.Tournaments\n            .Include(t => t.Players).ThenInclude(p => p.User)\n            .Include(t => t.Matches)\n            .Include(t => t.SeedTournament)\n            .Include(t => t.ParentTournament)\n            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(t => t.Id == query.TournamentId, ct);\n    }\n}\n",

PlayedAt = DateTime.UtcNow\n            };\n            _db.TournamentMatches.Add(match);\n        }\n\n        var team1Players = await GetOrCreatePlayers(tournament, command.Team1Player1Initials, _db.TournamentTeamMatchResults.AddRange(result1, result2);\n\n        match.State = MatchState.Done;\n        match.PlayedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;\n\n        await _db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);\n\n        // .ToListAsync(ct);\n\n        var playerIds = allTeamPlayers.SelectMany(t => t.TeamPlayers).Select(tp => tp.TournamentPlayerId).Distinct();\n        var players = await _db.TournamentPlayers.Where(p => playerIds.Contains(p.Id)).ToListAsync(ct);\n\n        var scoring = ScoringServiceFactory.Create(tournament.ScoreSystem);\n        scoring.Calculate(match, [result1, result2], allTeamPlayers, players);\n\n        foreach (var p in players)\n        {\n            p.MatchCount++;\n            if (match.State == MatchState.Done)\n            {\n                var result = result1.TeamId == allTeamPlayers.First(t => t.TeamPlayers.Any(tp => tp.TournamentPlayerId == p.Id)).Id\n                    ? result1 : result2;\n                var won = result.GoalsWon > result.GoalsLost;\n                if (won) p.WinCount++; else p.LoseCount++;\n                p.PointsWon += result.GoalsWon;\n                p.PointsLost += result.GoalsLost;\n            }\n        }\n\n        await _db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);\n        await _mediator.Publish(new MatchResultRecorded(match.Id, tournamentId), ct);\n        return match.Id;\n    }\n\n    private async Task<List<TournamentPlayer>> player = tournament.Players.FirstOrDefault(p => p.UserId == user.Id);\n        if (player != null) return player;\n\n        player = new TournamentPlayer\n        {\n            Id = Guid.NewGuid(),\n            UserId = user.Id,\n            TournamentId = tournament.Id,\n            Score = tournament.ScoreSystem == ScoreSystem.Lives ? 3 : 1000,\n            Lives = foreach (var tp in teamPlayers)\n        {\n            _db.TournamentTeamPlayers.Add(new TournamentTeamPlayer\n            {\n                TournamentTeamId = team.Id,\n                TournamentPlayerId = tp.Id\n            });\n        }\n\n        await _db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);\n        return team;\n    }\n}\n",

{
  "content": "using Idasletten.Features.Matches.Commands;\nusing Idasletten.Features.Users.Commands;\nusing Idasletten.Shared.Entities;\nusing Idasletten.Shared.Infrastructure;\nusing MediatR;\nusing Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;\n\nnamespace Idasletten.Features.Matches.Commands;\n\npublic class PlanSeveralMatchesHandler : IRequestHandler<PlanSeveralMatchesCommand, int>\n{\n    private readonly AppDbContext _db;\n    private readonly IMediator _mediator;\n\n    public PlanSeveralMatchesHandler(AppDbContext db, IMediator mediator)\n    {\n        _db = db;\n        _mediator = mediator;\n    }\n\n    public async Task<int> Handle(PlanSeveralMatchesCommand command, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var tournament = await _db.Tournaments\n            .Include(t => t.Players)\n            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(t => t.Id == command.TournamentId, ct)\n            ?? throw new InvalidOperationException(\"Tournament not found\");\n\n        var players = tournament.Players.ToList();\n        if (players.Count < 2) return 0;\n\n        var orderedPlayers = GetOrderedPlayers(command, players);\n        var totalMatches = (int)Math.Ceiling(players.Count * command.GamesPerPlayer / 2.0);\n        var matchesCreated = 0;\n\n        for (int i = 0; i < totalMatches; i++)\n        {\n            var (team1Players, team2Players) = PickTeams(orderedPlayers, command);\n\n            var match = new TournamentMatch\n            {\n                Id = Guid.NewGuid(),\n                TournamentId = command.TournamentId,\n                Order = await _db.TournamentMatches.CountAsync(m => m.TournamentId == command.TournamentId, ct) + 1,\n                State = MatchState.Planned,\n                CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow\n            };\n            _db.TournamentMatches.Add(match);\n            matchesCreated++;\n\n            var team1 = new TournamentTeam\n            {\n                Id = Guid.NewGuid(),\n                TournamentId = command.TournamentId,\n                Name = $\"Team {matchesCreated * 2 - 1}\",\n                Number = matchesCreated * 2 - 1\n            };\n            var team2 = new TournamentTeam\n            {\n                Id = Guid.NewGuid(),\n                TournamentId = command.TournamentId,\n                Name = $\"Team {matchesCreated * 2}\",\n                Number = matchesCreated * 2\n            };\n            _db.TournamentTeams.AddRange(team1, team2);\n\n            foreach (var p in team1Players)\n                _db.TournamentTeamPlayers.Add(new TournamentTeamPlayer { TournamentTeamId = team1.Id, TournamentPlayerId = p.Id });\n            foreach (var p in team2Players)\n                _db.TournamentTeamPlayers.Add(new TournamentTeamPlayer { TournamentTeamId = team2.Id, TournamentPlayerId = p.Id });\n        }\n\n        await _db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);\n        await _mediator.Publish(new SeveralMatchesPlanned(command.TournamentId, matchesCreated), ct);\n        return matchesCreated;\n    }\n\n    private List<TournamentPlayer> GetOrderedPlayers(PlanSeveralMatchesCommand command, List<TournamentPlayer> players)\n    {\n        if (command.SeedTournamentId.HasValue)\n        {\n            var seedPlayers = players.OrderByDescending(p => p.Score).ToList();\n            return command.SeedingType switch\n            {\n                SeedingType.Equality => OrderEquality(seedPlayers),\n                SeedingType.Fair => OrderFair(seedPlayers),\n                _ => Shuffle(seedPlayers)\n            };\n        }\n\n        return command.SeedingType switch\n        {\n            SeedingType.Equality => OrderEquality(players.OrderByDescending(p => p.Score).ToList()),\n            SeedingType.Fair => OrderFair(players.OrderByDescending(p => p.Score).ToList()),\n            _ => Shuffle(players)\n        };\n    }\n\n    private static List<TournamentPlayer> OrderEquality(List<TournamentPlayer> sorted)\n    {\n        var result = new List<TournamentPlayer>();\n        var count = sorted.Count;\n        for (int i = 0; i < count / 2; i++)\n        {\n            result.Add(sorted[i]);\n            result.Add(sorted[count - 1 - i]);\n        }\n        if (count % 2 == 1) result.Add(sorted[count / 2]);\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    private static List<TournamentPlayer> OrderFair(List<TournamentPlayer> sorted)\n    {\n        var count = sorted.Count;\n        var half = (count + 1) / 2;\n        var result = new List<TournamentPlayer>();\n        for (int i = 0; i < count / 2; i++)\n        {\n            result.Add(sorted[i]);\n            result.Add(sorted[half + i]);\n        }\n        if (count % 2 == 1) result.Add(sorted[half - 1]);\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    private static List<TournamentPlayer> Shuffle(List<TournamentPlayer> players)\n    {\n        var rng = new Random();\n        return players.OrderBy(_ => rng.Next()).ToList();\n    }\n\n    private static (List<TournamentPlayer>, List<TournamentPlayer>) PickTeams(\n        List<TournamentPlayer> ordered, PlanSeveralMatchesCommand command)\n    {\n        var teamSize = Math.Max(1, 2); // Default 2 per team\n        var team1 = ordered.Take(teamSize).ToList();\n        var team2 = ordered.Skip(teamSize).Take(teamSize).ToList();\n\n        if (team2.Count < teamSize) team2.Add(team1[0]); // fallback\n\n        if (!command.FixedTeams)\n        {\n            // Rotate for next match\n            var first = ordered[0];\n            ordered.RemoveAt(0);\n            ordered.Add(first);\n        }\n\n        return (team1, team2);\n    }\n}\n",

}

PlayedAt { get; set; }\n    public List<TeamViewModel> Teams { get; set; } = [];\n}\n\npublic class TeamViewModel\n{\n    public Guid Id { get; set; }\n    public string Name { get; set; } = null!;\n    public int Number { get; set; }\n    public int GoalsWon { get; set; }\n    public int GoalsLost { get; set; }\n    public List<string> PlayerInitials { get; set; } = [];\n}\n\npublic record GetMatchByIdQuery(Guid TournamentId, Guid MatchId) : IRequest<MatchViewModel?>;\n",

{
  "content": "using Idasletten.Features.Matches.Queries;\nusing Idasletten.Shared.Infrastructure;\nusing MediatR;\nusing Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;\n\nnamespace Idasletten.Features.Matches.Queries;\n\npublic class GetMatchesHandler :\n    IRequestHandler<GetMatchesForTournamentQuery, MatchesResult>,\n    IRequestHandler<GetMatchByIdQuery, MatchViewModel?>\n{\n    private readonly AppDbContext _db;\n\n    public GetMatchesHandler(AppDbContext db)\n    {\n        _db = db;\n    }\n\n    public async Task<MatchesResult> Handle(GetMatchesForTournamentQuery query, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var matches = await _db.TournamentMatches\n            .Include(m => m.TeamResults)\n                .ThenInclude(r => r.Team)\n                    .ThenInclude(t => t.TeamPlayers)\n                        .ThenInclude(tp => tp.Player)\n                            .ThenInclude(p => p.User)\n            .Where(m => m.TournamentId == query.TournamentId)\n            .OrderBy(m => m.Order)\n            .ToListAsync(ct);\n\n        var result = new MatchesResult();\n        foreach (var m in matches)\n        {\n            var vm = MapMatch(m);\n            if (m.State == Shared.Entities.MatchState.Planned)\n                result.Planned.Add(vm);\n            else\n                result.Completed.Add(vm);\n        }\n\n        return result;\n    }\n\n    public async Task<MatchViewModel?> Handle(GetMatchByIdQuery query, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var match = await _db.TournamentMatches\n            .Include(m => m.TeamResults)\n                .ThenInclude(r => r.Team)\n                    .ThenInclude(t => t.TeamPlayers)\n                        .ThenInclude(tp => tp.Player)\n                            .ThenInclude(p => p.User)\n            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(m => m.Id == query.MatchId && m.TournamentId == query.TournamentId, ct);\n\n        return match == null ? null : MapMatch(match);\n    }\n\n    private static MatchViewModel MapMatch(Shared.Entities.TournamentMatch m)\n    {\n        return new MatchViewModel\n        {\n            Id = m.Id,\n            Order = m.Order,\n            State = m.State.ToString(),\n            CreatedAt = m.CreatedAt,\n            PlayedAt = m.PlayedAt,\n            Teams = m.TeamResults.Select(r => new TeamViewModel\n            {\n                Id = r.TeamId,\n                Name = r.Team.Name,\n                Number = r.Team.Number,\n                GoalsWon = r.GoalsWon,\n                GoalsLost = r.GoalsLost,\n                PlayerInitials = r.Team.TeamPlayers\n                    .Select(tp => tp.Player.User.Username)\n                    .ToList()\n            }).ToList()\n        };\n    }\n}\n",

}

Name = null) : IRequest<Guid>;\n\npublic record PlayerAddedToTournament(Guid PlayerId, Guid TournamentId) : INotification;\n\npublic class AddPlayerToTournamentHandler : IRequestHandler<AddPlayerToTournamentCommand, Guid>\n{\n    private readonly AppDbContext _db;\n    private readonly IMediator _mediator;\n\n    public AddPlayerToTournamentHandler(AppDbContext db, IMediator mediator)\n    {\n        _db = db;\n        _mediator = mediator;\n    }\n\n    public async Task<Guid> Handle(AddPlayerToTournamentCommand command, CancellationToken ct)\n    {\n        var tournament = await _db.Tournaments.FindAsync([command.TournamentId], ct)\n            ?? throw new InvalidOperationException(\"Tournament not found\");\n\n        var user = await _mediator.Send(new CreateUserCommand(command.Initials, command.Name), ct);\n\n        var existing = await _db.TournamentPlayers\n            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(tp => tp.UserId == user.Id && tp.TournamentId == tournament.Id, ct);\n        if (existing != null) return existing.Id;\n\n        var player = new TournamentPlayer\n        {\n            Id = Guid.NewGuid(),\n            UserId = user.Id,\n            TournamentId = tournament.Id,\n            Score = tournament.ScoreSystem == ScoreSystem.Lives ? 3 : 1000,\n            Lives = tournament.ScoreSystem == ScoreSystem.Lives ? 3 : 0\n        };\n\n        _db.TournamentPlayers.Add(player);\n        await _db.SaveChangesAsync(ct);\n        await _mediator.Publish(new PlayerAddedToTournament(player.Id, tournament.Id), ct);\n        return player.Id;\n    }\n}\n",

  "content": "using System.Security.Claims;\nusing Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication;\nusing Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.Cookies;\nusing Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;\nusing Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.RazorPages;\n\nnamespace Idasletten.Pages;\n\npublic class LoginModel : PageModel\n{\n    private readonly IConfiguration _configuration;\n\n    public bool HasAzureAd { get; set; }\n    public bool HasTestUser { get; set; }\n    public string? ErrorMessage { get; set; }\n\n    public LoginModel(IConfiguration configuration)\n    {\n        _configuration = configuration;\n    }\n\n    public void OnGet()\n    {\n        HasAzureAd = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_configuration[\"AzureAd:ClientId\"]);\n        HasTestUser = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_configuration[\"TestUser:Email\"])\n            && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_configuration[\"TestUser:Password\"]);\n    }\n\n    public async Task<IActionResult> OnPostAzureLoginAsync()\n    {\n        return Challenge(new AuthenticationProperties { RedirectUri = \"/\" }, \"OpenIdConnect\");\n    }\n\n    public async Task<IActionResult> OnPostTestLoginAsync(string email, string password)\n    {\n        var configuredEmail = _configuration[\"TestUser:Email\"];\n        var configuredPassword = _configuration[\"TestUser:Password\"];\n\n        if (email != configuredEmail || password != configuredPassword)\n        {\n            ErrorMessage = \"Invalid test credentials.\";\n            HasAzureAd = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_configuration[\"AzureAd:ClientId\"]);\n            HasTestUser = true;\n            return Page();\n        }\n\n        var claims = new List<Claim>\n        {\n            new(ClaimTypes.Name, \"Test User\"),\n            new(ClaimTypes.Email, email),\n            new(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier, Guid.NewGuid().ToString())\n        };\n\n        var identity = new ClaimsIdentity(claims, CookieAuthenticationDefaults.AuthenticationScheme);\n        await HttpContext.SignInAsync(CookieAuthenticationDefaults.AuthenticationScheme, new ClaimsPrincipal(identity));\n\n        return RedirectToPage(\"/Index\");\n    }\n}\n",
  "filePath": "C:\\mjolner-code\\ai\\idasletten-llm-benchmark-v2\\idasletten-oc-deepseek-v4-pro\\Idasletten\\Pages\\Login.cshtml.cs"
}

Tournament { get; set; }\n    public List<Shared.Entities.Tournament> SeedTournaments { get; set; } = [];\n\n    public PlayersModel(IMediator mediator)\n    {\n        _mediator = mediator;\n    }\n\n    public async Task OnGetAsync(Guid tournamentId)\n    {\n        Tournament = await _mediator.Send(new GetTournamentByIdQuery(tournamentId));\n        SeedTournaments = await _mediator.Send(new GetTournamentsQuery());\n    }\n\n    public async Task<IActionResult> OnPostAddPlayerAsync(Guid tournamentId, string initials, string? name)\n    {\n        await _mediator.Send(new AddPlayerToTournamentCommand(tournamentId, initials, name));\n        return RedirectToPage(new { tournamentId });\n    }\n\n    public async Task<IActionResult> OnPostSeedFromTournamentAsync(Guid tournamentId, Guid seedTournamentId)\n    {\n        var seedTournament = await _mediator.Send(new GetTournamentByIdQuery(seedTournamentId));\n        if (seedTournament != null)\n        {\n            foreach (var seedPlayer in seedTournament.Players.OrderByDescending(p => p.Score))\n            {\n                await _mediator.Send(new AddPlayerToTournamentCommand(\n                    tournamentId, seedPlayer.User.Username, seedPlayer.User.Name));\n            }\n        }\n        return RedirectToPage(new { tournamentId });\n    }\n}\n",

opponent = new TournamentPlayer { Id = Guid.NewGuid(), Score = 1000 };\n\n        var teams = new List<TournamentTeam>\n        {\n            new() { Id = Guid.NewGuid(), TeamPlayers = [new() { TournamentTeamId = Guid.NewGuid(), TournamentPlayerId = player.Id }] },\n            new() { Id = Guid.NewGuid(), TeamPlayers = [new() { TournamentTeamId = Guid.NewGuid(), TournamentPlayerId = opponent.Id }] }\n        };\n\n        var results = new List<TournamentTeamMatchResult>\n        {\n            new() { TeamId = teams[0].Id, GoalsWon = 5, GoalsLost = 3 },\n            new() { TeamId = teams[1].Id, GoalsWon = 3, GoalsLost = 5 }\n        };\n\n        service.Calculate(new TournamentMatch(), results, teams, [player, opponent]);\n\n        Assert.True(player.Score > 1000);\n {\n            new() { Id = Guid.NewGuid(), TeamPlayers = [new() { TournamentTeamId = Guid.NewGuid(), TournamentPlayerId = player.Id }] },\n            new() { Id = Guid.NewGuid(), TeamPlayers = {
  "content": "# AGENTS.md — Idasletten\n\n## Tech Stack\n- **.NET 10.0**, Razor Pages, C#\n- **Entity Framework Core** + **SQLite** (in-memory for dev/testing, file-based for production)\n- **MediatR** for CQRS + vertical slice architecture\n- **basecoat UI** via CDN (`https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/basecoat@1/dist/basecoat.min.css`), includes Tailwind\n- **Azure AD** (OpenID Connect) for optional authentication\n- **Moserware.Skills** for TrueSkill scoring\n- **xUnit** for testing\n- **Fly.io** for deployment\n\n## Architecture: CQRS + Vertical Slices\n\n```\nIdasletten/\n  Features/\n    Matches/Commands/    — CreatePlannedMatch, RecordMatchResult, PlanSeveralMatches\n    Matches/Queries/     — GetMatchesForTournament, GetMatchById\n    Players/Commands/    — AddPlayerToTournament\n    Scoring/             — IScoringService, Elo, TrueSkill, Lives, WinCount\n    Tournaments/Commands/— CreateTournament\n    Tournaments/Queries/ — GetTournaments, GetPublicTournaments, GetTournamentById\n    Users/Commands/      — CreateUser\n    Users/Queries/       — GetUserByUsername, GetUserById\n  Pages/                 — Razor pages (thin controllers, only send MediatR commands/queries)\n  Shared/\n    Entities/            — EF Core entity classes + enums\n    Infrastructure/      — AppDbContext, DatabaseSeeder, DbContextExtensions\n```\n\n## Rules\n\n### Migrations\n- Use `dotnet ef migrations add <Name>` in the `Idasletten` project directory\n- Migrations auto-apply on startup (unless environment is \"Testing\")\n\n### Auth\n- Login is optional; recording match results works without login\n- `[Authorize]` required only for: creating tournaments, editing completed matches\n\n### Event Publishing\n- Every command handler MUST publish an `INotification` event at the end\n- Events: TournamentCreated, UserCreated, PlayerAddedToTournament, PlannedMatchCreated, SeveralMatchesPlanned, MatchResultRecorded\n\n### Handlers\n- Handlers use `AppDbContext` directly (no repository layer)\n- Handlers use `IMediator` for publishing events and sending nested commands\n\n### UI\n- basecoat UI via CDN (no local Tailwind config)\n- Light theme, Inter font, Flexbox for layout\n- Nordic/Norse mythology theme (dark red accent `#8b1a1a`)\n\n## Running Locally\n\n```bash\ncd Idasletten\ndotnet run\n```\n\nThe app runs at `http://localhost:5180` (http) or `https://localhost:7234` (https).\n\n### Test User\nSet environment variables to enable test login:\n- `TestUser__Email` — test user email\n- `TestUser__Password` — test user password\n\n## Running Tests\n\n```bash\ndotnet test\n```\n\nTests use `WebApplicationFactory<Program>` with an in-memory database (unique per test run).\n\n## Deployment\n\n```bash\nfly deploy\n```\n\nOr push to `main` to trigger the GitHub Actions workflow.\n\n## Test Conventions\n- Method naming: `Should_DoSomething_When_ConditionIsFulfilled`\n- AAA pattern (Arrange / Act / Assert)\n- `Any.*` static factory for test data\n- Prefer simple stubs over mocking frameworks\n",

}

## Assistant (Build · DeepSeek V4 Pro · 14.2s)

_Thinking:_

Ambiguous reference between `Idasletten.Pages.IndexModel` and `Idasletten.Pages.Tournaments.IndexModel`. I added `@using Idasletten.Pages.Tournaments` to ViewImports which causes this conflict. The fix is to either:
1. Use fully qualified model name in each .cshtml or
2. Remove the `@using Idasletten.Pages.Tournaments` and `@using Idasletten.Pages.Users` and `@using Idasletten.Pages.Account` from ViewImports.

Option 2 is better since the Razor engine should automatically find the model class in the same directory. The original issue was that `IndexModel` wasn't being found at all... but that's because the generated code couldn't find it without the using. Let me reconsider.

Actually, the issue is that `@using Idasletten.Pages` brought in the root IndexModel but also `@using Idasletten.Pages.Tournaments` brought in the Tournaments IndexModel. Both have `IndexModel`.

The cleanest fix: remove the extra usings from ViewImports and just use fully qualified names in cshtml files where needed, OR rename the Tournaments.IndexModel to something unique.

Actually, the better approach: just remove the problematic imports. The Razor engine will automatically resolve page models in the same directory. The issue was that IndexModel couldn't be found before, but that was actually because the Namespace might not have been right... wait, no. Let me think again.

The first build error said `IndexModel could not be found` - this was because the ViewImports didn't have `@using Idasletten.Pages`. Now with `@using Idasletten.Pages`, it can find it but it's ambiguous with Tournaments' IndexModel.

Solution: Remove `@using Idasletten.Pages.Tournaments` and `@using Idasletten.Pages.Users` and `@using Idasletten.Pages.Account` from ViewImports. Keep only `@using Idasletten.Pages`. The model resolution in Razor should work by convention (matching by file location).

Let me try this.

{
  "content": "using Idasletten.Shared.Entities;\nusing Moserware.Skills;\nusing Moserware.Skills.TrueSkill;\n\nnamespace Idasletten.Features.Scoring;\n\npublic class TrueSkillScoringService : IScoringService\n{\n    private readonly GameInfo _gameInfo = GameInfo.DefaultGameInfo;\n\n    public void Calculate(\n        TournamentMatch match,\n        IReadOnlyList<TournamentTeamMatchResult> teamResults,\n        IReadOnlyList<TournamentTeam> teams,\n        IReadOnlyList<TournamentPlayer> players)\n    {\n        var team1Result = teamResults[0];\n        var team2Result = teamResults[1];\n        var team1 = teams.Single(t => t.Id == team1Result.TeamId);\n        var team2 = teams.Single(t => t.Id == team2Result.TeamId);\n\n        var team1Players = GetTeamPlayers(team1, players);\n        var team2Players = GetTeamPlayers(team2, players);\n\n        var team1MoserPlayers = team1Players.Select(p => new Moserware.Skills.Player(p.Id)).ToList();\n        var team2MoserPlayers = team2Players.Select(p => new Moserware.Skills.Player(p.Id)).ToList();\n\n        var defaultRating = _gameInfo.DefaultRating;\n\n        var moserTeam1 = new Moserware.Skills.Team();\n        foreach (var p in team1MoserPlayers)\n            moserTeam1.AddPlayer(p, defaultRating);\n\n        var moserTeam2 = new Moserware.Skills.Team();\n        foreach (var p in team2MoserPlayers)\n            moserTeam2.AddPlayer(p, defaultRating);\n\n        int rank1 = team1Result.GoalsWon >= team2Result.GoalsWon ? 1 : 2;\n        int rank2 = team2Result.GoalsWon >= team1Result.GoalsWon ? 1 : 2;\n\n        var newRatings = TrueSkillCalculator.CalculateNewRatings(\n            _gameInfo,\n            Teams.Concat(moserTeam1, moserTeam2),\n            rank1, rank2);\n\n        foreach (var (player, moserPlayer) in team1Players.Zip(team1MoserPlayers))\n        {\n            var newRating = newRatings[moserPlayer];\n            double newScore = (newRating.Mean - 3 * newRating.StandardDeviation) * 100;\n            player.ScoreDiff = newScore - player.Score;\n            player.Score = newScore;\n        }\n\n        foreach (var (player, moserPlayer) in team2Players.Zip(team2MoserPlayers))\n        {\n            var newRating = newRatings[moserPlayer];\n            double newScore = (newRating.Mean - 3 * newRating.StandardDeviation) * 100;\n            player.ScoreDiff = newScore - player.Score;\n            player.Score = newScore;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static List<TournamentPlayer> GetTeamPlayers(TournamentTeam team, IReadOnlyList<TournamentPlayer> allPlayers)\n    {\n        return team.TeamPlayers\n            .Select(tp => allPlayers.Single(p => p.Id == tp.TournamentPlayerId))\n            .ToList();\n    }\n}\n",
  "filePath": "C:\\mjolner-code\\ai\\idasletten-llm-benchmark-v2\\idasletten-oc-deepseek-v4-pro\\Idasletten\\Features\\Scoring\\TrueSkillScoringService.cs"
}

  "content": "using Idasletten.Shared.Infrastructure;\nusing Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Testing;\nusing Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;\nusing Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;\nusing Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection.Extensions;\nusing Microsoft.Extensions.Hosting;\n\nnamespace Idasletten.Tests;\n\npublic class IdaslettenWebApplicationFactory : WebApplicationFactory<Program>\n{\n    private readonly string _connectionString = $\"DataSource={Guid.NewGuid()};Mode=Memory;Cache=Shared\";\n\n    protected override IHost CreateHost(IHostBuilder builder)\n    {\n        builder.UseEnvironment(\"Testing\");\n\n        builder.ConfigureServices(services =>\n        {\n            services.RemoveAll(typeof(AppDbContext));\n            services.RemoveAll(typeof(DbContextOptions<AppDbContext>));\n\n            services.AddDbContext<AppDbContext>(options =>\n                options.UseSqlite(_connectionString));\n        });\n\n        return base.CreateHost(builder);\n    }\n\n    protected override void ConfigureClient(HttpClient client)\n    {\n        base.ConfigureClient(client);\n\n        using var scope = Services.CreateScope();\n        var db = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<AppDbContext>();\n        db.Database.OpenConnection();\n        db.Database.EnsureCreated();\n    }\n\n    public async Task SeedData(Action<AppDbContext> seedAction)\n    {\n        using var scope = Services.CreateScope();\n        var db = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<AppDbContext>();\n        seedAction(db);\n        await db.SaveChangesAsync();\n    }\n}\n",

{
  "content": "using Idasletten.Shared.Infrastructure;\nusing Microsoft.AspNetCore.Hosting;\nusing Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Testing;\nusing Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;\nusing Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;\n\nnamespace Idasletten.Tests;\n\npublic class IdaslettenWebApplicationFactory : WebApplicationFactory<Program>\n{\n    protected override void ConfigureWebHost(IWebHostBuilder builder)\n    {\n        builder.UseEnvironment(\"Testing\");\n\n        builder.ConfigureServices(services =>\n        {\n            var descriptorsToRemove = services\n                .Where(d =>\n                    d.ServiceType == typeof(DbContextOptions<AppDbContext>) ||\n                    d.ServiceType == typeof(AppDbContext) ||\n                    d.ServiceType.FullName?.Contains(\"EntityFrameworkCore\") == true)\n                .ToList();\n\n            foreach (var d in descriptorsToRemove)\n                services.Remove(d);\n\n            services.AddDbContext<AppDbContext>(options =>\n            {\n                options.UseInMemoryDatabase($\"idasletten-test-{Guid.NewGuid()}\");\n            });\n        });\n    }\n\n    public void SeedData(Action<AppDbContext> seedAction)\n    {\n        using var scope = Services.CreateScope();\n        var db = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<AppDbContext>();\n        db.Database.EnsureCreated();\n        seedAction(db);\n    }\n}\n",
  "filePath": "C:\\mjolner-code\\ai\\idasletten-llm-benchmark-v2\\idasletten-oc-deepseek-v4-pro\\Idasletten.Tests\\IdaslettenWebApplicationFactory.cs"
}

## Assistant (Build · DeepSeek V4 Pro · 11.1s)

_Thinking:_

All tasks are complete. Let me provide a summary of what was built.

All tasks complete. Here's the summary:

## Idasletten — Table Football Tournament App

**Built at:** `idasletten-oc-deepseek-v4-pro`

### What was built

- **.NET 10.0 Razor Pages** project with **CQRS + MediatR** vertical slice architecture
- **SQLite** database with EF Core, auto-migrations on startup
- **4 scoring systems**: Elo (`K=32`, team average), TrueSkill (Moserware.Skills), Lives (3 lives, lose on loss), WinCount (score = wins)
- **Feature slices** — Tournaments, Matches, Players, Users (commands + queries with event publishing)
- **11 Razor Pages** — Home, Login, Tournaments (Index, Create, Detail, CreateMatch, Matches, MatchDetail, Players), Users/Profile
- **basecoat UI** via CDN with Nordic theme (dark red `#8b1a1a` accent)
- **Test login** via `TestUser__Email` / `TestUser__Password` env vars
- **Azure AD** optional authentication
- **Deployment**: Dockerfile, `fly.toml`, GitHub Actions workflow

### Verification

- **Build:** passes (warnings only for Moserware.Skills compatibility shim)
- **9 tests pass** (6 scoring unit tests + 3 integration tests)
- **11 Playwright screenshots** in `screenshots/` covering all pages
- **End-to-end validated:** creating tournaments, recording match results, test login, score updates

### Running locally

```bash
cd Idasletten
$env:TestUser__Email = "test@test.com"
$env:TestUser__Password = "test123"
dotnet run
```
